			else

			end

		end

	end,

			New = function(p78)

					end
				end)

						t15:Close()
					end)
				end

				return t15
			end,
		}

	u = function()
		local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
		local v409 = handlers.load("b")
		local New = v409.New
		local t97 = {
			New = function(p127, p128, p129, p130)
				local Frame = New("Frame", {
					Size = UDim2.new(0, p130, 1, 0),

					Active = true,

					Parent = v412,
				})
				local u414 = false
				local n3 = 0

				local function v416()
					local Y = p127.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y
					local Y2 = p127.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y

					if Y <= Y2 then
						v412.Visible = false

						return
					end

					v412.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, math.clamp(Y2 / Y, 0.1, 1), 0)
					v412.Visible = true
				end
				local function v419()
					local Scale = v412.Position.Y.Scale
					local v421 = math.max(p127.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y - p127.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y, 0)

					if v421 <= 0 then
						return
					end

					local v422 = math.max(1 - v412.Size.Y.Scale, 0)

					if v422 <= 0 then
						return
					end

					p127.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(p127.CanvasPosition.X, Scale / v422 * v421)
				end
				local function v423()
					if u414 then
						return
					end

					local Y = p127.CanvasPosition.Y
					local v425 = math.max(p127.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y - p127.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y, 0)

					if v425 <= 0 then
						v412.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

						return
					end

					local v426 = math.max(1 - v412.Size.Y.Scale, 0)

					v412.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, math.clamp(Y / v425 * v426, 0, v426), 0)
				end

					if
						p131.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
						or p131.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
					then
						local Y = v412.AbsolutePosition.Y

						if not (p131.Position.Y >= Y and p131.Position.Y <= Y + v412.AbsoluteSize.Y) then
							local Y2 = v412.AbsoluteSize.Y

							v412.Position = UDim2.new(
								0,
								0,
								math.clamp(
									(p131.Position.Y - Frame.AbsolutePosition.Y - Y2 / 2) / (Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y - Y2),
									0,
									1 - v412.Size.Y.Scale
								),
								0
							)
							v419()
						end
					end
				end)
				v409.AddSignal(Frame2.InputBegan, function(p132)
					if
						p132.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
						or p132.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch

								input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
								or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
							then

								end

								if connection then
									connection:Disconnect()
								end
							end
						end)
					end
				end)

					v416()

	v = function()
		local v432 = handlers.load("b")
		local New = v432.New
		local Tween = v432.Tween

		local function v435(p133)
			local R = p133.R
			local G = p133.G
			local B = p133.B
			local v439 = math.max(R, G, B)
			local v440 = v439 - math.min(R, G, B)
			local n4

			if v440 == 0 then
				n4 = 0
			else
				local v442

				if v439 == R then
					v442 = (G - B) / v440 % 6
				elseif v439 == G then
					v442 = (B - R) / v440 + 2
				else
					v442 = (R - G) / v440 + 4
				end

				n4 = v442 * 60
			end

			local v443 = v439 == 0 and 0 or v440 / v439

			return {
				h = math.floor(n4 + 0.5),
				s = v443,
				b = v439,
			}
		end
		local function v444(p134)
			return 0.299 * p134.R + 0.587 * p134.G + 0.114 * p134.B
		end
		local function v445(p135)
			local h = v435(p135).h

			if v444(p135) > 0.5 then
				return Color3.fromHSV(h / 360, 0, 0.05)
			end

			return Color3.fromHSV(h / 360, 0, 0.98)
		end
		local function v447(p136)
			local Keypoints = p136.Color.Keypoints
			local n5 = 0
			local n6 = 0
			local n7 = 0

			for _, Keypoint in ipairs(Keypoints) do
				n5 += Keypoint.Value.R
				n6 += Keypoint.Value.G
				n7 += Keypoint.Value.B
			end

			local v454 = #Keypoints

			return Color3.new(n5 / v454, n6 / v454, n7 / v454)
		end

		local t98 = {
			New = function(p137, p138, p139)
				local t99 = {
					Title = p138.Title or "Tag",
					Icon = p138.Icon,

				t99.Color = p138.Color or Color3.fromHex("#315dff")
				t99.Radius = p138.Radius or 999
				t99.TagFrame = nil
				t99.Height = 26
				t99.Padding = 10
				t99.TextSize = 14
				t99.IconSize = 16

				local v456

				if t99.Icon then
					v456 = v432.Image(t99.Icon, t99.Icon, 0, p138.Window, "Tag", false)
					v456.Size = UDim2.new(0, t99.IconSize, 0, t99.IconSize)
					v456.ImageLabel.ImageColor3 = typeof(t99.Color) == "Color3" and v445(t99.Color) or nil
				end

				local TextLabel = New("TextLabel", {
					BackgroundTransparency = 1,
					AutomaticSize = "XY",
					TextSize = t99.TextSize,
					FontFace = Font.new(v432.Font, Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
					Text = t99.Title,
					TextColor3 = typeof(t99.Color) == "Color3" and v445(t99.Color) or nil,
				})
				local UIGradient

				if typeof(t99.Color) == "table" then
					UIGradient = New("UIGradient")

					for k, v in next, t99.Color, nil do
						UIGradient[k] = v
					end

					TextLabel.TextColor3 = v445(v447(UIGradient))

					if v456 then
						v456.ImageLabel.ImageColor3 = v445(v447(UIGradient))
					end
				end

				local NewRoundFrame = v432.NewRoundFrame
				local Radius = t99.Radius
				local t100 = {
					AutomaticSize = "X",
					Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, t99.Height),
					Parent = p139,
					ImageColor3 = typeof(t99.Color) == "Color3" and t99.Color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
				}
				local UIPadding = New("UIPadding", {
					PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, t99.Padding),
					PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, t99.Padding),
				})
				local t102 = {
					FillDirection = "Horizontal",
					VerticalAlignment = "Center",
					Padding = UDim.new(0, t99.Padding / 1.5),
				}
				local t101 = {

				}

					local values = table.pack(New("UIListLayout", t102))

					table.move(values, 1, values.n, 5, t101)
				end

				local squircle = NewRoundFrame(Radius, "Squircle", t100, t101)

				t99.SetTitle = function(p140, p141)
					t99.Title = p141
					TextLabel.Text = p141
				end
				t99.SetColor = function(p142, p143)
					t99.Color = p143

					if typeof(p143) == "table" then
						local v469 = v447(p143)

						Tween(TextLabel, 0.06, {
							TextColor3 = v445(v469),
						}):Play()

						local UIGradient2 = squircle:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
							or New("UIGradient", {
								Parent = squircle,

							UIGradient2[k] = v

							ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
						}):Play()

						if UIGradient then
							UIGradient:Destroy()
						end

							TextColor3 = v445(p143),
						}):Play()

								ImageColor3 = v445(p143),
							}):Play()
						end

						Tween(squircle, 0.06, {
							ImageColor3 = p143,
						}):Play()
					end
				end

			end,

	w = function()
		local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
		local u474
		local t103

		t103 = {
			Folder = nil,
			Path = nil,
			Configs = {},
			Parser = {
				Colorpicker = {

						return {

				Dropdown = {
					Save = function(p147)

							__type = p147.__type,

							p148:Select(p149.value)
						end
					end,
				},
				Input = {
					Save = function(p150)
						return {
							__type = p150.__type,
							value = p150.Value,
						}
					end,
					Load = function(p151, p152)
						if p151 and p151.Set then
							p151:Set(p152.value)
						end
					end,
				},
				Keybind = {
					Save = function(p153)
						return {
							__type = p153.__type,
							value = p153.Value,
						}
					end,
					Load = function(p154, p155)
						if p154 and p154.Set then
							p154:Set(nil, p155.value)
						end
					end,
				},
				ToggleKeybind = {
					Save = function(p156)
						return {
							__type = p156.__type,
							value = p156.Value,
							key = p156.Key,
						}
					end,
					Load = function(p157, p158)
						if p157 and p157.Set then
							p157.Set(nil, p158.value, true)
						end

						if p157 and p157.SetKey and p158.key then
							p157.SetKey(nil, p158.key)
						end
					end,
				},
				Slider = {
					Save = function(p159)
						return {
							__type = p159.__type,
							value = p159.Value.Default,
						}
					end,
					Load = function(p160, p161)
						if p160 and p160.Set then
							p160:Set(tonumber(p161.value))
						end
					end,
				},
				Toggle = {
					Save = function(p162)
						return {
							__type = p162.__type,
							value = p162.Value,
						}
					end,
					Load = function(p163, p164)
						if p163 and p163.Set then
							p163:Set(p164.value)
						end
					end,
				},
			},
			Init = function(p165, p166)
				if not p166.Folder then
					warn("[ WindUI.ConfigManager ] Window.Folder is not specified.")

					return false
				end

				u474 = p166
				t103.Folder = u474.Folder
				t103.Path = "WindUI/" .. tostring(t103.Folder) .. "/config/"

				if not isfolder("WindUI/" .. t103.Folder) then
					makefolder("WindUI/" .. t103.Folder)

					if not isfolder("WindUI/" .. t103.Folder .. "/config/") then
						makefolder("WindUI/" .. t103.Folder .. "/config/")
					end
				end

				for _, v in next, t103:AllConfigs(), nil do
					if isfile and readfile and isfile(v .. ".json") then
						t103.Configs[v] = readfile(v .. ".json")
					end
				end

				return t103
			end,
			CreateConfig = function(p167, p168)
				local t104 = {
					Path = t103.Path .. p168 .. ".json",
					Elements = {},
					CustomData = {},
					Version = 1.1,
				}

				if not p168 then
					return false, "No config file is selected"
				end

				t104.SetAsCurrent = function()
					u474:SetCurrentConfig(t104)
				end
				t104.Register = function(p169, p170, p171)
					t104.Elements[p170] = p171
				end
				t104.Set = function(p172, p173, p174)
					t104.CustomData[p173] = p174
				end
				t104.Get = function(p175, p176)
					return t104.CustomData[p176]
				end
				t104.Save = function()
					if u474.PendingFlags then
						for k, v in next, u474.PendingFlags, nil do
							t104:Register(k, v)
						end
					end

					local t105 = {
						__version = t104.Version,
						__elements = {},
						__custom = t104.CustomData,
					}

					for k, v in next, t104.Elements, nil do
						if t103.Parser[v.__type] then
							t105.__elements[tostring(k)] = t103.Parser[v.__type].Save(v)
						end
					end

					local json = HttpService:JSONEncode(t105)

					if writefile then
						writefile(t104.Path, json)
					end

					return t105
				end
				t104.Load = function()
					if isfile and not isfile(t104.Path) then
						return false, "Config file does not exist"
					end

					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						return HttpService:JSONDecode((readfile or function()
							warn("[ WindUI.ConfigManager ] The config system doesn't work in the studio.")

							return nil
						end)(t104.Path))
					end)

					if not ok then
						return false, "Failed to parse config file"
					end

					local v487

					if result.__version then
						v487 = result
					else
						v487 = {
							__version = t104.Version,
							__elements = result,
							__custom = {},
						}
					end

					if u474.PendingFlags then
						for k, v in next, u474.PendingFlags, nil do
							t104:Register(k, v)
						end
					end

					local __elements = v487.__elements or {}

					for k, v in next, __elements, nil do
						if t104.Elements[k] and t103.Parser[v.__type] then
							task.spawn(function()
								t103.Parser[v.__type].Load(t104.Elements[k], v)
							end)
						end
					end

					t104.CustomData = v487.__custom or {}

					return t104.CustomData
				end
				t104.Delete = function()
					if not delfile then
						return false, "delfile function is not available"
					end

					if not isfile(t104.Path) then
						return false, "Config file does not exist"
					end

					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						delfile(t104.Path)
					end)

					if not ok then
						return false, "Failed to delete config file: " .. tostring(result)
					end

					t103.Configs[p168] = nil

					if u474.CurrentConfig == t104 then
						u474.CurrentConfig = nil
					end

					return true, "Config deleted successfully"
				end
				t104.GetData = function()
					return {
						elements = t104.Elements,
						custom = t104.CustomData,
					}
				end
				t104:SetAsCurrent()
				t103.Configs[p168] = t104

				return t104
			end,
			DeleteConfig = function(p177, p178)
				if not delfile then
					return false, "delfile function is not available"
				end

				local v495 = t103.Path .. p178 .. ".json"

				if not isfile(v495) then
					return false, "Config file does not exist"
				end

				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					delfile(v495)
				end)

				if not ok then
					return false, "Failed to delete config file: " .. tostring(result)
				end

				t103.Configs[p178] = nil

				if u474.CurrentConfig and u474.CurrentConfig.Path == v495 then
					u474.CurrentConfig = nil
				end

				return true, "Config deleted successfully"
			end,
			AllConfigs = function()
				if not listfiles then
					return {}
				end

				local t106 = {}

				if not isfolder(t103.Path) then
					makefolder(t103.Path)

					return t106
				end

				local v499, v500 = listfiles(t103.Path)

				for _, v in next, v499, v500 do
					local match = v:match("([^\\/]+)%.json$")

					if match then
						table.insert(t106, match)
					end
				end

				return t106
			end,
			GetConfig = function(p179, p180)
				return t103.Configs[p180]
			end,
		}

		return t103
	end,

		local function v558(p190)
			local R = p190.R
			local G = p190.G
			local B = p190.B
			local v562 = math.max(R, G, B)
			local v563 = v562 - math.min(R, G, B)
			local n8

			if v563 == 0 then
				n8 = 0
			else
				local v565

				if v562 == R then
					v565 = (G - B) / v563 % 6
				elseif v562 == G then
					v565 = (B - R) / v563 + 2
				else
					v565 = (R - G) / v563 + 4
				end

			end

			local v566 = v562 == 0 and 0 or v563 / v562

				h = math.floor(n8 + 0.5),
				s = v566,
				b = v562,
			}
		end

		end

		end

				end

		local function v1016(p301)
			local t229 = {}

			for _, v in ipairs(p301) do
				t229[v] = true
			end

			return t229
		end

		local v1020 = v1016(t227.lua)
		local v1021 = v1016(t227.rbx)
		local v1022 = v1016(t227.operators)

		local function v1023(p302, p303)
			local v1024 = p302[p303]

			if t228[v1024 .. "_color"] then
				return t228[v1024 .. "_color"]
			end

			if tonumber(v1024) then
				return t228.numbers
			end

			if v1024 == "nil" then
				return t228.null
			end

			if v1024:sub(1, 2) == "--" then
				return t228.comment
			end

			if v1022[v1024] then
				return t228.operator
			end

			if v1020[v1024] then
				return t228.lua
			end

			if v1021[v1024] then
				return t228.rbx
			end

			if v1024:sub(1, 1) == '"' or v1024:sub(1, 1) == "'" then
				return t228.str
			end

			if v1024 == "true" or v1024 == "false" then
				return t228.boolean
			end

			if p302[p303 + 1] == "(" then
				if p302[p303 - 1] == ":" then
					return t228.self_call
				end

				return t228.call
			end

			if p302[p303 - 1] ~= "." then
				return
			end

			if p302[p303 - 2] == "Enum" then
				return t228.rbx
			end

			return t228.local_property
		end

		local t226 = {
			run = function(p304)
				local t230 = {}
				local v1026 = false
				local v1027 = false
				local s2 = ""
				local v1029 = false

				for i = 1, #p304 do
					local v1031 = p304:sub(i, i)

					if v1026 then
						if v1031 == "\n" and not v1027 then
							table.insert(t230, s2)
							table.insert(t230, v1031)
							v1026 = false
							s2 = ""
						elseif p304:sub(i - 1, i) == "]]" and v1027 then
							table.insert(t230, s2 .. "]")
							v1026 = false
							v1027 = false
							s2 = ""
						else
							s2 ..= v1031
						end
					elseif v1029 then
						if v1031 == v1029 and p304:sub(i - 1, i - 1) ~= "\\" or v1031 == "\n" then
							s2 ..= v1031
							v1029 = false
						else
							s2 ..= v1031
						end
					elseif p304:sub(i, i + 1) == "--" then
						table.insert(t230, s2)
						v1027 = p304:sub(i + 2, i + 3) == "[["
						v1026 = true
						s2 = "-"
					elseif v1031 == '"' or v1031 == "'" then
						table.insert(t230, s2)
						s2 = v1031
						v1029 = v1031
					elseif v1022[v1031] then
						table.insert(t230, s2)
						table.insert(t230, v1031)
						s2 = ""
					elseif v1031:match("[%w_]") then
						s2 ..= v1031
					else
						table.insert(t230, s2)
						table.insert(t230, v1031)
						s2 = ""
					end
				end

				table.insert(t230, s2)

				local t231 = {}

				for i, v in ipairs(t230) do
					local v1035 = v1023(t230, i)

					if v1035 then
						local v1036 = string.format(
							'<font color = "#%s">%s</font>',
							v1035:ToHex(),
							v:gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;")
						)

						table.insert(t231, v1036)
					else
						table.insert(t231, v)
					end
				end

				return table.concat(t231)
			end,
		}

		return t226
	end,

							local v1599 = v1590(v2.Title or "", p431)
							local v1600 = v1590(v2.Desc or "", p431)

							if v1599 or v1600 then
								t384[k2] = {
									Title = v2.Title,
									Desc = v2.Desc,
									Original = v2,
									__type = v2.__type,
									Index = k2,
								}
							end
						end
					end

					if v1595 or next(t384) ~= nil then
						t383[k] = {
							Tab = v,
							Title = v.Title,
							Icon = v.Icon,
							Elements = t384,
						}
					end
				end

				return t383
			end

			local function v1696(p437)
				local match = p437:match("%.(%w+)$") or p437:match("%.(%w+)%?")

				if match then
					local v1698 = match:lower()

					if v1698 == "jpg" or v1698 == "jpeg" or v1698 == "png" or v1698 == "webp" then
						return "." .. v1698
					end
				end

				return ".png"
			end

						local uDim22 = UDim2.new(
							uDim2.X.Scale,
							math.clamp(uDim2.X.Offset, t385.MinSize.X, t385.MaxSize.X),
							uDim2.Y.Scale,
							math.clamp(uDim2.Y.Offset, t385.MinSize.Y, t385.MaxSize.Y)
						)

						Tween(t385.UIElements.Main, 0, {
							Size = uDim22,
						}):Play()
						t385.Size = uDim22
					end
				end
			end)

			local n16 = 0
			local n17 = 0.4
			local u1897
			local n18 = 0

			onDoubleClick = function()
				t385:SetToTheCenter()
			end
			squircle.Frame.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
				local now = tick()
				local Position2 = t385.Position

				n18 += 1

				if n18 == 1 then
					n16 = now
					u1897 = Position2
					task.spawn(function()
						task.wait(0.4)

						if n18 == 1 then
							n18 = 0
							u1897 = nil
						end
					end)
				elseif n18 == 2 then
					if now - n16 <= n17 and Position2 == u1897 then
						onDoubleClick()
					end

					n18 = 0
					u1897 = nil
					n16 = 0
				else
					n18 = 1
					n16 = now
					u1897 = Position2
				end
			end)

			if not t385.HideSearchBar then
				local v1901 = handlers.load("W")
				local u1902 = false
				local Search = New2("Search", "search", t385.UIElements.SideBarContainer, true)

				Search.Size = UDim2.new(1, -t385.UIPadding / 2, 0, 39)
				Search.Position = UDim2.new(0, t385.UIPadding / 2, 0, t385.UIPadding / 2)
				v1614.AddSignal(Search.MouseButton1Click, function()
					if u1902 then
						return
					end

					v1901.new(t385.TabModule, t385.UIElements.Main, function()
						u1902 = false

						if t385.Resizable then
							t385.CanResize = true
						end

						Tween(v1627, 0.1, {
							ImageTransparency = 1,
						}):Play()
						v1627.Active = false
					end)
					Tween(v1627, 0.1, {
						ImageTransparency = 0.65,
					}):Play()
					v1627.Active = true
					u1902 = true
					t385.CanResize = false
				end)
			end

			t385.DisableTopbarButtons = function(p511, p512)
				for _, v in next, p512, nil do
					for _, v2 in next, t385.TopBarButtons, nil do
						if v2.Name == v then
							v2.Object.Visible = false
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return t385
		end
	end,
}

local windUI = {
	Window = nil,
	Theme = nil,
	Creator = handlers.load("b"),
	LocalizationModule = handlers.load("c"),
	NotificationModule = handlers.load("d"),
	Themes = nil,
	Transparent = false,
	TransparencyValue = 0.15,
	UIScale = 1,
	ConfigManager = nil,
	Version = "0.0.0",
	Services = handlers.load("h"),
	OnThemeChangeFunction = nil,
}
local data = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(handlers.load("i"))

if data then
	windUI.Version = data.version
end

local v1910 = handlers.load("m")
local Creator = windUI.Creator
local New = Creator.New
local v1913 = handlers.load("q")

if game:GetService("Players") then
	local LocalPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
end

local protectgui_ = protectgui or syn and syn.protect_gui or function() end
local hui = gethui and gethui() or game.CoreGui or game.Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local t457 = {
	New("UIScale", {
		Scale = windUI.Scale,
	}),
	New("Folder", {
		Name = "Window",
	}),
	New("Folder", {
		Name = "KeySystem",
	}),
	(New("Folder", {
		Name = "Popups",
	})),
}

do
	local values = table.pack(New("Folder", {
		Name = "ToolTips",
	}))

	table.move(values, 1, values.n, 5, t457)
end

windUI.ScreenGui = New("ScreenGui", {
	Name = "WindUI",
	Parent = hui,
	IgnoreGuiInset = true,
	ScreenInsets = "None",
}, t457)
windUI.NotificationGui = New("ScreenGui", {
	Name = "WindUI/Notifications",
	Parent = hui,
	IgnoreGuiInset = true,
})
windUI.DropdownGui = New("ScreenGui", {
	Name = "WindUI/Dropdowns",
	Parent = hui,
	IgnoreGuiInset = true,
})
protectgui_(windUI.ScreenGui)
protectgui_(windUI.NotificationGui)
protectgui_(windUI.DropdownGui)
Creator.Init(windUI)
math.clamp(windUI.TransparencyValue, 0, 1)

local v1919 = windUI.NotificationModule.Init(windUI.NotificationGui)

windUI.Notify = function(p513, p514)
	p514.Holder = v1919.Frame
	p514.Window = windUI.Window

	return windUI.NotificationModule.New(p514)
end
windUI.SetNotificationLower = function(p515, p516)
	v1919.SetLower(p516)
end
windUI.SetFont = function(p517, p518)
	Creator.UpdateFont(p518)
end
windUI.OnThemeChange = function(p519, p520)
	windUI.OnThemeChangeFunction = p520
end
windUI.AddTheme = function(p521, p522)
	windUI.Themes[p522.Name] = p522

	return p522
end
windUI.SetTheme = function(p523, p524)
	if not windUI.Themes[p524] then
		return nil
	end

	windUI.Theme = windUI.Themes[p524]
	Creator.SetTheme(windUI.Themes[p524])

	if windUI.OnThemeChangeFunction then
		windUI.OnThemeChangeFunction(p524)
	end

	return windUI.Themes[p524]
end
windUI.GetThemes = function()
	return windUI.Themes
end
windUI.GetCurrentTheme = function()
	return windUI.Theme.Name
end
windUI.GetTransparency = function()
	return windUI.Transparent or false
end
windUI.GetWindowSize = function()
	return Window.UIElements.Main.Size
end
windUI.Localization = function(p525, p526)
	return windUI.LocalizationModule:New(p526, Creator)
end
windUI.SetLanguage = function(p527, p528)
	if Creator.Localization then
		return Creator.SetLanguage(p528)
	end

	return false
end
windUI.ToggleAcrylic = function(p529, p530)
	if windUI.Window and windUI.Window.AcrylicPaint and windUI.Window.AcrylicPaint.Model then
		windUI.Window.Acrylic = p530
		windUI.Window.AcrylicPaint.Model.Transparency = p530 and 0.98 or 1

		if p530 then
			v1913.Enable()
		else
			v1913.Disable()
		end
	end
end
windUI.Gradient = function(p531, p532, p533)
	local t458 = {}
	local t459 = {}

	for k, v in next, p532, nil do
		local v1924 = tonumber(k)

		if v1924 then
			local v1925 = math.clamp(v1924 / 100, 0, 1)

			table.insert(t458, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(v1925, v.Color))
			table.insert(t459, NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(v1925, v.Transparency or 0))
		end
	end

	table.sort(t458, function(p534, p535)
		return p534.Time < p535.Time
	end)
	table.sort(t459, function(p536, p537)
		return p536.Time < p537.Time
	end)

	if #t458 < 2 then
		error("ColorSequence requires at least 2 keypoints")
	end

	local t460 = {
		Color = ColorSequence.new(t458),
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new(t459),
	}

	if p533 then
		for k, v in pairs(p533) do
			t460[k] = v
		end
	end

	return t460
end
windUI.Popup = function(p538, p539)
	p539.WindUI = windUI

	return handlers.load("r").new(p539)
end
windUI.Themes = handlers.load("s")(windUI)
Creator.Themes = windUI.Themes
windUI:SetTheme("Dark")
windUI:SetLanguage(Creator.Language)
windUI.CreateWindow = function(p540, p541)
	local v1929 = handlers.load("X")

	if not isfolder("WindUI") then
		makefolder("WindUI")
	end

	if p541.Folder then
		makefolder(p541.Folder)
	else
		makefolder(p541.Title)
	end

	p541.WindUI = windUI
	p541.Parent = windUI.ScreenGui.Window

	if windUI.Window then
		warn("You cannot create more than one window")

		return
	end

	Creator.SetTheme(windUI.Themes[p541.Theme or "Dark"])

	local v1930 = (gethwid or function()
		return game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.UserId
	end)()

	if p541.KeySystem then
		local u1931 = false

		local function v1932()
			v1910.new(p541, v1930, function(p542)
				u1931 = p542
			end)
		end

		local v1933 = (p541.Folder or "Temp") .. "/" .. v1930 .. ".key"

		if not p541.KeySystem.API then
			if p541.KeySystem.SaveKey and isfile(v1933) then
				local v1934 = readfile(v1933)

				if
					type(p541.KeySystem.Key) == "table" and table.find(p541.KeySystem.Key, v1934)
					or tostring(p541.KeySystem.Key) == tostring(v1934)
				then
					u1931 = true
				else
					v1932()
				end
			else
				v1932()
			end
		elseif isfile(v1933) then
			local v1935 = readfile(v1933)
			local v1936 = false

			for _, v in next, p541.KeySystem.API, nil do
				local v1939 = windUI.Services[v.Type]

				if v1939 then
					local t461 = {}

					for _, v2 in next, v1939.Args, nil do
						table.insert(t461, v[v2])
					end

					if v1939.New(table.unpack(t461)).Verify(v1935) then
						v1936 = true

						break
					end
				end
			end

			u1931 = v1936

			if not v1936 then
				v1932()
			end
		else
			v1932()
		end

		repeat
			task.wait()
		until u1931
	end

	local v1943 = v1929(p541)

	windUI.Transparent = p541.Transparent
	windUI.Window = v1943

	if p541.Acrylic then
		v1913.init()
	end

	return v1943
end

return windUI
